# Disposition of every original test

The starting inventory contains 2,823 methods. Each row below records its implemented destination or retirement. Retired integrations are represented by family lifecycles and primitive/contract checks; source-call overlap alone does not prove semantic or branch coverage equivalence. The numerical matrix deliberately replaces a 223-way execution cross-product with exhaustive specifications and 19 calculation representatives. Accepted in the fresh full run: 639 units, 635 passes, four existing skips, 762.295 seconds.
